**Authority and scope (2026-10-15):** Resolve the 25-failure verification gap and exercise inactive Doot, purchased-schematic cost omission, and unknown cost operands through actual production components. No production or permanent test changes were authorized. The temporary browser route and scripts were removed; private run and differential artifacts remain.

#### Domain red check and attribution

- The recorded command remains **red**: `yarn test tests/domains/hole/majik.test.ts tests/domains/hole/schematics.test.ts tests/domains/hole/measurements.test.ts --runInBand`, exit **1**, **3 suites / 213 tests; 188 passed / 25 failed**. I searched the retained execution history and private artifacts, but could not recover the complete original per-test Jest output. Do not treat this as green or hide it.
- `yarn tsx .ui-verification/hole-input-pairing.cjs` compared retained `paired_hole_inputs` with `latest.json`; `node .ui-verification/hole-assertion-diagnostic.cjs` compared imported public fields with retained values. The three extraction files share paired-input SHA-256 `53daec4ef4fe5930032574c54dba2e4e486e15b54a2eb3f328ce4266c21ab708`. Pairing comparison found **four mismatched inputs**: Hole levels expected `[20,48,68,30,150]`, current `[20,49,69,30,151]`; Hole EXP arrays differ; study-progress indices 11–13 differ; wishes expected **3397**, current **3427**. These are independent diagnostics, not original Jest output; they do not claim one-to-one mapping with its 25 failed tests.
- Isolated before/after normal-import differential command: `yarn tsx .ui-verification/hole-cost-differential.cjs before && yarn tsx .ui-verification/hole-cost-differential.cjs after`. Separate processes imported unchanged dependencies and `latest.json`; before mode changed only the Fountain currency image assignment in an in-memory module copy. Before currency image was `null`; after it was `{location:"HoleSilverCoins",width:23,height:28}`. No checkout, revert, save, fixture, dependency, test, or extraction mutation occurred.
- The differential excludes calculation-output changes from the selected currency metadata. The other selected changes are display-only component rendering; this normal-import check never loads them. Paired-input mismatch evidence and output equality establish the 25 recorded failures as pre-existing to these three display changes, not a reason to alter current expectations. No new formula, authored effect, or audit panel is needed.

### Part 2 phase 5 fresh review after H40–H41

**Authority and method (2026-10-15):** Fresh independent, read-only review covers selected part 2 [#442](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/442), its published plan approval, the unchanged [backend plan](hole-part-2-backend-plan.md), and the approved specification. Read coding standards, contributor guidance, domain guidance, Hole vocabulary, architecture guidance, UI guidance for the affected Research display, and the phase 5 workflow. Isolated `code-review` axes ran serially: `HolePart2ConsumerFreshReview.HoleConsumerStandards`, then `HolePart2ConsumerFreshReview.HoleConsumerSpec`. Plan approval was not treated as a standards exception.

**Comparison scope:** Reviewed the complete selected uncommitted backend against `HEAD` (`31f1513731defa60a854125390f268924bcd9db9`), not only H40–H41. Coverage includes Hole/Villager/Gambit, registration, Companions, Divinity, Tome, Farming, Orion/Poppy, relevant Research backend/display changes, eight selected-part suites and helper, the changed part 1 pipeline suite, four config/result pairs, and affected consumers. Supplied generated building/currency exports were read-only prerequisites. Unrelated Research/Spelunking work and the unrelated Divinity blessing test edit were not attributed to this revision.

#### Standards

**Zero new findings.** No additional documented breach or actionable maintainability smell was confirmed. H36–H39 retain aspect-owned progression, boundary translation, prepared Doot eligibility, and missing-schematic meaning. H40's nullable state follows a genuine optional-input dependency; initialized owners remain required. The narrow Research contributor type preserves numeric contracts for independent contributors. No speculative engine, frontend layout contract, unnecessary absence guard, or manufactured effect description was added. `Unavailable` is a missing-value status, not replacement game-mechanic prose.

#### Specification

**Zero new selected findings. H40–H41 are resolved within their inspected contracts.** Measurement 13 now supplies its nullable effective benefit to Gambit. Dependent scores, unlocks, rewards, Orion/Poppy inputs, Orion production, and Research offline totals retain unavailable state. Saved records, independent production/contributors, Tutorial-gated rewards, and known zeroes remain distinct. Research's existing display handles the changed contract without new layout or interaction; this is necessary consumer migration under plan lines 65 and 74, not another feature's UI delivery. No rendered-behavior claim is added.

Divinity owns eligible ordinary activation. Pocket consumes that authority for overlap and effective membership. Secondary eligibility requires a primary link and an unlocked secondary god. Effective membership deduplicates ordinary links, prepared Doot, and Pocket. Primary-only Gaming remains separate. Early shared effects and late inspection retain their distinct roles. Descriptions remain generated text with value substitution. No forecast, allocation model, navigation redesign, or main-stat engine was introduced.

#### Remaining Divinity failures: disposition, not dismissal

- **Nobisect blessing, expected 1070 / received 0 — confirmed pre-existing code defect.** Current `data/domain/world-5/divinity.tsx:63–67` returns zero for god 2. Direct inspection of that file at `HEAD` shows the same branch. The retained original `hole-part2-god-callers-1.22.json`, `result[0].source`, contains the nonzero `BlesssBonus` rule. The fixture has blessing level 214; generated metadata supplies 50 per level; the source's lower multiplier bound is 0.1. This supports the retained 1070 result. The Divinity page still calls `getBlessingBonusText()` (`app/world-5/divinity/content.tsx:146`), so the existing defect is real, not a stale zero/nonzero oracle. H40–H41 do not change this calculation. No fix is authorized here.
- **Active AGI, expected 2024359 / received 2024269 — incompatible comparison inputs, not a demonstrated Hole regression.** `tests/results/divinity-blessing-data.json:27–29` captures live `TotalStats("AGI")`. `tests/fixtures/saves/latest.json:11335` stores 2024269 in `PVStatList_3[1]`. `data/domain/player.tsx:767`, `918–919`, and `71–75` copy that snapshot value directly. The parameter test at `tests/domains/divinity/blessing-parameters.test.ts:77–83` compares this imported field with the live calculated output. No selected code recalculates main stats; the normal-import assertions explicitly preserve them. This explains the received value and rules out an introduced stat mutation. Why the live calculation differs by 90 remains an unresolved Divinity capture/contract prerequisite; the result file has no same-context pairing metadata. Do not declare its numeric oracle stale, alter expectations, or add a Hole stat engine to make it pass.

#### Standards

**Zero findings. H36–H38 resolved.** `CosmoUpgrade.prepare` owns individual progression. Parsing translates numeric investments, including placeholders, and predecessor state before calculation. Pocket inspection consumes prepared Doot eligibility. No further documented breach or actionable maintainability smell was found. No scope expansion, speculative framework, frontend layout contract, authored effect description, or impossible initialized-record absence guard was introduced. #### Specification

- **H40 — P2: Missing Measurement inputs lose availability in Gambit.** `data/domain/world-5/hole/hole.tsx:1966` supplies unavailable Death Note points when optional character `KLA_` input is missing. `Measurement.prepare` records unavailable statistics but stores a zero fallback at line 922. Measurement 13 correctly exposes `effectiveBenefit = null`. The Gambit consumer at lines 1982–1987 still calls `getBonus()`, which uses that fallback and returns the purchased base benefit with multiplier one. It publishes a numeric multiplier, total score, and unlock decisions despite the missing dependency (`gambit.tsx:145–153`). The plan, line 65, requires: **“A genuinely missing optional save input makes only dependent values unavailable.”** Its integration also requires migrating affected consumers when an API changes. **Recommendation for decision:** Preserve Measurement availability through the affected Gambit results and consumers. Keep independent saved challenge progress and verified zeroes. Add a narrow real-input omission/recovery contract using existing test conventions. This is a static code-path finding, not an observed game branch.
- **H41 — P2: Pocket overlap counts an ineligible secondary link as ordinary activation.** `hole.tsx:263–281` includes every saved `esGod` in `ordinaryGodIds`, then uses that set for overlap, added gods, and effective gods. Captured original [`hole-part2-god-callers-1.22.json`](../../.ui-verification/hole-part2-backend-20261014/hole-part2-god-callers-1.22.json), `result[0].source`, shows the secondary `Bonus_MAJOR` path requires a primary link and an unlocked secondary god (`Divinity[25] > selected god ID`). Without effective Doot, an ineligible saved secondary selection therefore appears active. If Pocket selects that god, inspection calls it overlap instead of a new activation. The plan, line 61, requires: **“Preserve secondary-link rules and callers that count only primary links.”** **Recommendation for decision:** Obtain eligible ordinary activation from its Divinity owner. Keep the saved secondary identity separate, then calculate Pocket overlap from effective links. Existing secondary parsing predates this patch; this finding concerns the new inspection records, not a claimed legacy parsing regression. The paired Doot context does not exercise this branch.

**Specification verdict:** Two P2 findings remain, H40–H41. H39 is resolved for its selected missing-schematic-row contract. Otherwise, inspected formulas and retained comparisons support schematic order/currency/prices, the shared Majik pool/caps/effects, and all 17 Measurement mappings and benefits. Early preparation precedes required consumers; late inspection does not feed shared calculations. Descriptions use generated text and current-value substitution. No UI, forecast, allocation model, or new character-stat engine was added.

### Part 2 phase 5 independent backend review

**Authority and method (2026-10-15):** Fresh, read-only review covers selected part 2 [#442](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/442), its published approval, the [backend plan](hole-part-2-backend-plan.md), and the [approved specification](hole-spec-draft.md). Followed phase 5 of the [feature workflow](../human/feature-workflow.md), `code-review`, architecture guidance, [CODING_STANDARDS.md](../../CODING_STANDARDS.md), [CONTRIBUTING.md](../../CONTRIBUTING.md), domain discovery, and existing Hole vocabulary. Isolated axes ran serially: `HolePart2BackendReview.HoleP2Standards`, then `HolePart2BackendReview.HoleP2Spec`. Plan approval was not treated as a standards exception.

**Comparison scope:** Baseline `HEAD` is `31f1513731defa60a854125390f268924bcd9db9`. Selected backend changes are uncommitted. Reviewed `git diff HEAD --` for Hole, Villager, pipeline registration, Divinity, Companions, Tome, Farming, and the affected part 1 pipeline test. Also reviewed the eight new selected-part suites, helper, extraction configs/results, and relevant producer/consumer contracts. Supplied generated building/currency exports were read-only prerequisite context, not attributed to the implementation worker. Unrelated Research, Spelunking, navigation, generated exports, and unrelated test edits were excluded.

#### Standards

- **H36 — P2, MODEL-01: Majiks owns individual upgrade progression behavior.** `data/domain/world-5/hole/hole.tsx:314–326` computes and writes each `CosmoUpgrade`'s initial/enhancement levels, access, cap, predecessor state, and purchase eligibility inside `Majiks.prepare`. The upgrade stores these fields but cannot prepare its own progression. A rule change spans both the container and responsible concept. [MODEL-01](../../CODING_STANDARDS.md#model-01--model-responsible-concepts) requires aspect update logic and game calculations on the aspect class; the plan's responsibilities at lines 21–22 do not waive that rule. **Recommendation for decision:** Let `CosmoUpgrade` prepare its progression from the pool, access, gate, and predecessor inputs supplied by `Majiks`.
- **H37 — P2, PARSE-01: Raw Majik tree encoding survives parsing.** `hole.tsx:293–297` retains the original save arrays in `investments`. Calculation later converts their values with `Number` at line 308 and reads predecessors through `[tree][treePosition - 1]` at line 322. The calculation still depends on raw save layout, despite parsed upgrade records. This violates [PARSE-01](../../CODING_STANDARDS.md#parse-01--translate-at-the-boundary) and the plan's line 35 boundary. **Recommendation for decision:** Translate predecessor state and complete numeric investment inputs during parsing, including undisplayed placeholder investments. Calculate from named state rather than retained save arrays.
- **H38 — P3, non-blocking smell judgement: Pocket inspection repeats the Doot eligibility rule.** `hole.tsx:235–241` recomputes companion ownership plus the character's Divinity-level gate. `data/domain/companions.tsx:121–125` already prepares that rule as `PlayerDivinityInfo.dootActive`, which `data/domain/world-5/divinity.tsx:222–226` consumes. Late Pocket inspection runs after both updates. This is **Duplicated Code / possible Feature Envy**, not a separate hard violation. Two production owners must now maintain the same gate. **Recommendation for decision:** Consume prepared eligibility for existing Divinity records; retain genuine missing-save handling.

**Standards verdict:** Two documented P2 breaches remain, plus one P3 maintainability judgement. No further substantiated scope expansion, speculative framework, frontend-shaped state, manufactured description, or impossible initialized-object absence guard was found.

#### Specification

- **H39 — P2: Missing schematic input becomes a false zero Contented Creator benefit.** `hole.tsx:1467` counts built flags and supplies a plain numeric total to `Majiks.prepare`. If optional `Holes[13]` is absent while Village Majik investment exists, `Schematic.parse` sets all flags false (`352–354`). `CosmoUpgrade.getEffects` then exposes a known `0%` benefit (`163–164`), and `getDescription` substitutes that false total (`187`). Saved Majik progression remains known; its schematic-dependent benefit does not. Existing villager growth already marks this dependency unavailable (`1620,1644,1671`). The backend plan, line 65, requires: **“A genuinely missing optional save input makes only dependent values unavailable. Retain independent progression and known zeroes.”** The product specification, line 48, likewise says **“Unknown inputs must not become zero.”** **Recommendation for decision:** Preserve construction-count availability and leave the dependent effect/placeholder unavailable without hiding saved Majik progression. Current complete-save tests do not exercise this path.

**Specification verdict:** One P2 defect remains. Apart from H39, inspected implementation and assertions support released schematic order/availability, full currency joins, current price formulas, the shared Majik pool and caps, Pocket deduplication, and all 17 Measurement mappings, units, and formulas. Early shared preparation precedes affected consumers; late inspection does not feed shared calculations. Tome and Farming producer changes serve required numeric statistics, not another feature. Descriptions derive from generated static text and verified placeholder materialization. No new UI, allocation model, forecast, character-stat engine, or unrelated feature was introduced.

The final Jest JSON records **25 passed suites / 773 passed tests / 0 failed tests**. The selected eight suite counts total **263**. Initial failures and their recorded corrections remain historical evidence.

Assertions retain saved progression, imported main stats, character binding, stable derived records, and affected Hole/Divinity/Companion/Votes/Equinox outputs. Producer comparisons cover required numeric Tome, crop, Death Note, damage, Slab, Study, Lengthmeister, and Fountain inputs.
- **Sufficiency:** Retained evidence supports personalized parity for one matched real save and original offline Jelly_Operator v1.22, active Player 3 only. Pairing records all **799** fixture keys equal. Eleven imported character bindings do not establish eleven active runtime contexts. Source inspection supports rules, not unobserved personalized parity.

### Phase 8 amended UI independent review

**Authority and scope (2026-10-14):** Reviewed selected part 1 [#441](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/441) in a fresh independent session. The current amended [UI plan](hole-part-1-ui-plan.md), its [publication](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/441#issuecomment-6160185301), and approved rendered captures govern this review. Earlier owner-link, URL-selection, contributor-table, and automatic scroll/focus requirements are superseded. Followed the scoped UI guide, `code-review`, Impeccable context, `CODING_STANDARDS.md`, `CONTRIBUTING.md`, and domain vocabulary discovery. Standards and specification assessments used separate agents in serial order. Approval was not treated as a standards exception.

Reviewed current uncommitted production content with `git diff f9efbcf5 -- app/world-5/hole/content.tsx`. The reviewed content SHA-256 is `e0193d66c398b7caf71e9ad72e1bad1f07f66792cd881424ee1d2e8e61f88902`, matching the retained production evidence source. The route wrapper and both accepted Hole backend sources have no diff from `41f29730`. Unrelated Research and Spelunking work is excluded.

#### Standards

- **H31 — P3, CONSIST-01: Labeled row fields bypass DataRow's label ownership.** `content.tsx:220–223,275–276` uses `kind: "content", label: null` for Level, Invested Opals, stored progress, and rate fields, then renders labels inside `TextAndLabel`. The row guide reserves `label: null` for genuinely unlabeled content (`ui-rows.md:16–17`) and assigns compact label/value typography to DataRow (`24–26`). The UI guide also distinguishes wrappers outside DataRow from its private row rendering (`ui-patterns.md:101–104`). The result is readable and matches the approved preview, but shared row-label changes will not reach these fields. Use text fields for simple values and labeled content fields for genuinely rich values. Keep the selected progression grouping and tooltip behavior. The plan's stronger value hierarchy does not exempt field composition from the shared contract.
- **H32 — P3, DATA-01 / SIMPLE-01: UI revalidates guaranteed Statue contributor metadata.** `content.tsx:52–56` checks contributor count, owner encoding, value identity, unit, and combination. `Villager.prepareCharacterRates` always appends exactly one Statue contributor with those fixed fields (`villager.tsx:341–369`), including when saved Statue input is missing. This duplicates the producer contract in the view and makes a metadata change require another maintenance step. Remove guaranteed-structure validation, not genuine missing-value/reason checks or the complete numeric comparison required by the plan. The typed-input `typeof` checks and the post-completeness null-input recheck are redundant defenses in the same concern. Finite-value checks, nullable saved inputs, and the local account-loading guard remain justified.

#### Specification

- **H33 — P2: Useful nonzero growth modifiers are no longer inspectable.** The whitelist at `content.tsx:201–209` retains only purchased EXP, role multiplier, Equal Spread, Opal Enthusiasm, and the bundle, besides the separately shown Statue. All other shared growth sources disappear, with no surviving shared context. The approved plan says **“Keep useful nonzero modifiers”** (`ui-plan.md:17`) and retains **“useful advanced context”** in decision 5 (`70`). Real-save records include Bravery **8,314.38%**, Grimoire **172.5%**, Arcade **9.23%**, and other material contributors. The full-size `rate-0-rounded.png` shows that none appear. Players can read the rate but cannot inspect those sources. Preserve useful nonzero shared modifiers once in concise tooltip context. Do not restore repeated contributor tables, owner metadata, zeros, or neutral multipliers. The approved preview contains the same omission; visual agreement alone does not satisfy the written outcome.

#### All fifteen presentation decisions

| Decision | Assessment and evidence |
|---|---|
| 1. Concise game terms | Met. Progression, participation, and benefit copy no longer exposes owner, intrinsic, scope, or combination labels. |
| 2. Toolbox priorities only | Met. Adjacent values, recognizable art, and benefit clarity carry over. No competitor topology, colors, ETA sorting, cards, or total rate was added. |
| 3. Incumbent colors | Met. Retained style records and captures show brand-blue content tabs, accent-2 labels, accent-4 rail selection, and charcoal panels. |
| 4. No deep links or source surfaces | Met. Router/query state, owner destinations, source panels, return links, and feature anchors are removed. The retained legacy-query record proves local selection does not write the URL. |
| 5. Advanced rate tooltips | Partial: H33. Growth details buttons are removed and conventional tooltips work, but useful shared modifiers are discarded. |
| 6. Deduplication | Met for repeated descriptions/effect tables, zero source/location rows, neutral modifiers, repeated status, and contributor inventories. H33 identifies over-pruning, not a request to restore those inventories. |
| 7. Value hierarchy | Visually met. Aligned progression groups and stronger values match the approved render. H31 remains a separate shared-row standards issue. |
| 8. Statue-bound character meaning | Met in the supported evidence. Complete unrounded records agree across all 55 villager/character contexts, yielding immediate rates for all 11 imported characters. Differing/unavailable fixtures retain truthful ranges, optional named inspection, matched Statue context, and visible missing-input reasons. No account-wide rate claim appears. |
| 9. Useful Lantern locations | Met. Stock is 37, storage is the only nonzero baseline location, uses are 0 / 12, and the effect is +2 hours. Missing location inputs remain unavailable. Alternate/zero/missing locations are view fixtures. |
| 10. No wiki/manual prose | Met. Only concise progression, benefit, reset, and exclusion context remains. |
| 11. No automatic scroll/focus | Met. Source has no destination effects. All 38 retained native-click transitions preserve actual shared scrollers or record natural height clamping and blur. |
| 12. Players/obols rails | Visually met. Both Hole rails use a padded 25% column, shared selection arrow/background, and dark-1 panel. Reference locator timeouts remain explicit below. |
| 13. Verified art | Met. All five CDN banners loaded at 303×64 and clip their verified 56×64 artwork region. Opal/Lantern metadata is reused. Unverified cavern thumbnails are not guessed. |
| 14. Local-only cavern benefits | Met. All 18 cavern identities remain reachable and show only their own Study benefit, local earned Opals, and one website-support notice. |
| 15. Contextual numeric benefits | Met. The 18 Study rows preserve values, relevant conditions, and missing reasons without divisor/unit/scope schema. Cross-skilling arithmetic stays in tooltips and does not claim a literal resource reduction. |

### Phase 8 independent UI review

**Authority and scope (2026-10-14):** Independently reviewed selected part 1 [#441](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/441), its published UI-plan comment, the [approved UI plan](hole-part-1-ui-plan.md), canonical IA, approved preview, and phase 7 production evidence. Followed phase 8, `code-review`, the scoped UI guide, `CODING_STANDARDS.md`, `CONTRIBUTING.md`, and domain vocabulary discovery. Standards and specification reviewers used isolated contexts and ran serially, as required for this assignment. Plan approval was not treated as a standards exception.

The fixed point and current `HEAD` both resolve to `41f29730dfa83650d34180c33923b3a1d4087fc9`. There are no intervening commits. The three-dot commit diff is therefore empty; `git diff 41f29730 -- app/world-5/hole/content.tsx components/navigation.tsx` supplies the uncommitted production comparison. Reviewed both current files and their relevant accepted domain/store/shared-component contracts. Unrelated dirty and untracked work stayed excluded.

#### Standards

- **H27 — P3, LANG-01: Player-facing details expose implementation terms instead of their meaning.** `app/world-5/hole/content.tsx:269-271` first translates the effect type, then prints raw `owned-study-contribution` and `owned-study-rule` scope tokens. It also renders domain notes verbatim. Actual Fountain detail `12-study-16-detail-expanded.png` says “The delivered marbleization caller checks upgrade exclusions without a Study check.” Wisdom detail `12-study-13-detail-expanded.png` refers to “The round caller.” Cove detail and its owner viewport repeat raw scope tokens. Other authored labels expose “typed effects,” “The backend floors,” and raw owner tokens (`content.tsx:286,338,370,396,430`). These require knowledge of implementation internals, contrary to LANG-01. Show readable scope and source names. Explain the established player-facing restrictions without caller/backend terminology. Preserve the divisor rule, Wisdom capacity condition, and Fountain owned-effect versus full eligibility distinction; do not replace them with a stronger game-benefit claim or change accepted calculations.
- **H28 — P3, DATA-01: The saved-time object has an impossible absence branch.** `content.tsx:171` casts `timeAway` as `| undefined` and uses `?.GlobalTime` after the loaded-data gate. `data/domain/idleonData.tsx:419` creates that map entry unconditionally; line `423` directly accesses the parsed object's `GlobalTime` before the import completes. `lib/stores/appDataStore.tsx:191-192,207-208` publishes `StaticData`/`LiveData` only after that import. The outer object is therefore required on this path. Remove only its artificial optionality. Retain checks for a genuinely missing or invalid saved timestamp field. This is a maintainability finding, not a demonstrated wrong timestamp.
- **H29 — P3, READ-01: The Study effect field combines too many view responsibilities in one expression.** `content.tsx:393` combines the effect loop, two semantic-caveat branches, detail selection/navigation, and the owner link inside one field expression. Lines `276-277` similarly mix pinned-owner inclusion, access filtering, description materialization, effect-label search, and normalization. Correct behavior still leaves these independently meaningful rules difficult to inspect or change. Use explicit named view steps or a small local summary renderer. No new generic framework, options layer, or broad component migration is needed. This finding concerns responsibility clarity, not line length or formatter-enforced style.

#### Specification

This is source-confirmed missing destination presentation; the exact post-click viewport consequence is inferred from the code and retained composition, not freshly reproduced. The retained scenario checks `target.waitFor({ state: 'visible' })` at `scenario.cjs:136-145,353-361,492-494`; Playwright visibility permits offscreen elements. Those passing assertions establish rendered/query selection, not owner discoverability. Bring the selected owner into view after rendering, including direct query links and reload, without changing the catalogue or adding a new navigation model.

- **Mechanic source:** Read-only `POST http://127.0.0.1:3101/exec` with JSON `{ "expression": "..." }` returned original **Jelly_Operator 1.22**. - **Character-context contract:** The Statue multiplier affects every villager's growth rate. Different character Statue state can therefore produce valid differences for identical shared Hole progression. Join each rate and contributor set through existing Player ID/name and matching PlayerStatues. Identify that character's Statue percentage and factor separately from account contributors. Expose no arbitrary, best, first, or active primary rate, and no unavailable account-rate field solely because rates disagree. No imported game active-character selector was established. A genuinely missing required input affects only dependent context values; retain independent information and a reason, never fake zero. Keep initialized required objects required. Later UI must label inspection explicitly, such as **Rates for [character name]** or **Inspected character: [name]**. Inspection describes the imported snapshot, not game selection, activity, or assignment changes. Widgets and default inspection selection remain future UI decisions. ### Phase 5 independent backend review

**Authority and scope (2026-10-13):** Reviewed selected part 1, [#441](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/441), against the amended backend plan. Followed [phase 5](../human/feature-workflow.md#5-review-the-backend-independently), `CODING_STANDARDS.md`, `CONTRIBUTING.md`, and domain vocabulary discovery. Reviewed uncommitted Hole work against `HEAD` (`989abd2d`), including all six new suites/configs/results and their helper. Standards and specification reviews used separate reviewers, delegated serially. Unrelated Research, Deathnote, Gambit, and other feature edits were not classified as Hole scope expansion.

#### Standards

- **H1 — P2, MODEL-01: Villager does not own its complete growth preparation.** `data/domain/world-5/hole/hole.tsx:1199-1276` computes role-specific growth rules, availability, and contributor records. `villager.tsx:185-198` receives those results and performs the final multiplication. Polonai and Cosmo role rules therefore remain in the parent. This splits one aspect's behavior across two owners, despite the plan's Villager responsibility. Keep shared inputs and allocation in Hole. Put role-specific preparation and contributor construction on Villager; no new role framework is needed.
- **H2 — P3, DATA-01: Required Pristine Charm has an impossible absence fallback.** `hole.tsx:1590,1621` uses `pristineCharm?.unlocked` to substitute zero when the record is absent. Sneaking creates every delivered Pristine Charm during parsing (`world-6/sneaking.tsx:457-466`). Candy Cache, item ID 21, is a required catalogue record (`data/NinjaItemRepo.ts:837-845`). Treat that record as required. Keep the real unowned-charm branch, not a catalogue-absence branch.
- **H3 — P3, UPDATE-01: Early shared preparation includes display-only calculations.** `hole.tsx:1051-1068` prepares next intrinsic unlocks and the Another Try inspection summary in `postProcessingMap` (`idleonData.tsx:273`). These outputs have no calculation consumers. The standard places UI-only calculations in `postPostProcessingMap`. Keep early Study/schematic inputs and Tome's cavern total early. Prepare unlock/reset inspection data in the existing late Hole step.

These are documented-standard findings, not heuristic smell claims. No generic modifier framework, new account-data model, excessive test seam, or independently actionable abstraction smell was confirmed.

#### Specification

- **H4 — P2: Unknown Superbit ownership becomes inactive neighbor rates.** `hole.tsx:1617` passes a Boolean ownership value without saved-input availability. `gaming/palette.ts:41-49` defaults absent `Gaming[12]` to unpurchased. With a known target and accessible neighbors, Hole then publishes no eligible neighbors and zero neighbor accrual. This violates outcome 3 and the plan's missing-input rule. Preserve the known target rate. Mark only potentially eligible neighbor participation/rates unavailable when ownership input is genuinely missing.
- **H5 — P2: Missing Majik state becomes zero allocation effects.** `hole.tsx:1173-1184` checks villager inputs but not the required Village Majik state. Parsing absent `Holes[5]` assigns Majik levels zero (`1402-1404`). Equal Spread and Opal Enthusiasm then report zero with no missing-input reason, although growth rates correctly become unavailable. This violates outcome 2. Preserve known least investment and invested tens. Make the dependent percentages unavailable when their Majik input is missing.
- **H6 — P2: Missing card save still produces complete growth rates.** The growth availability list (`hole.tsx:1376-1387`) omits `Cards0`, while growth consumes Gloomie card level (`1196,1625`). Cards defaults an absent save to `{}` and missing counts to zero (`cards.tsx:228-247`). A genuinely absent card save can therefore publish numeric rates and a zero card contributor with no reason. This violates outcome 1. Track card-save availability and mark dependent rates/contributions unavailable; retain a real zero from a present save.
- **H7 — P2: Cross-skilling effect names describe the wrong numeric meaning.** `Study.getEffects()` (`hole.tsx:379-385`) labels `bonus * completedLayers` as a percentage requirement reduction. Captured values are 570%, 1870%, 3420%, and 972%. Original `Holes/TotalOreREQ` divides requirements by `1 + (collectible bonus + summed Study contributions) / 100`. These values are additive denominator bonuses, not literal percentage reductions. The config's `study_effects_1/7/11/16` expressions reproduce that intermediate arithmetic, so passing tests do not validate the label. Name the owned divisor contribution accurately and explain its additive combination. Do not claim a complete cavern requirement or invent a new calculator.

H4–H6 are source-proven missing-input branches, not exercised runtime failures. H7 concerns the observed API values and their source-backed meaning. The approved plan does not exempt these contracts from standards or permit unknown inputs to become zero.

#### Evidence assessment and check limits

- The retained comparisons support all five growth rates for Player 3, direct parameters, ledger reconciliation, and the observed Study/Lantern state. Other characters have identity/Statue binding checks, not captured game-output parity. Fresh/repeated imports use the same save; they do not prove changed-account replacement.
- Reviewed early Study/schematic ordering, late settled account/Player/Statue inputs, Tome's cavern-only contract, and all caller migrations. No additional ordering failure was demonstrated. Full ledger attribution and the separate Emperor/schematic floor adjustment follow the inspected refresh caller.
- Study 15 correctly separates owned descriptive text from cavern permission. Original `CanWeBUY_Marbleize` does not check Study ownership. Wisdom's extra caller condition is recorded. Hand-composed effect oracles remain limited evidence for consumer semantics, as H7 shows.
- Jar initialization and Farming's Exotic Market upgrade 51 fix support required inputs and stay within the approved plan. No Hole UI, forecast, simulation, recommendation, store, endpoint, or game action was added. Five-role, 18-Study, and all-character collection coverage serves the approved outcomes; the test count alone is not an excess finding.
- This review read code, diffs, retained artifacts, and original source-function strings from the documented offline endpoint. It ran no tests, builds, lint, formatters, new calculation extraction, game actions, character switches, or time advancement. Phase 4's reported 17 suites / 427 tests, type check, and scoped lint were not rerun. Its test files were ignored by lint. The unobserved branches listed in the continuation result remain unobserved.
- **Decision boundary:** Four specification findings and three standards findings await decisions. Code stayed read-only. Only this handoff changed. No fix, acceptance, tracker publication, commit, push, PR, deployment, or issue closure occurred.

### Phase 5 fresh backend review

**Authority and scope (2026-10-13):** Independently reviewed selected part 1, [#441](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/441), against the amended backend plan and its two published plan comments. Reviewed current uncommitted work against `HEAD` (`989abd2d`), including new tests/configs/results and required Sneaking catalogue initialization. Followed `code-review`, phase 5, `CODING_STANDARDS.md`, `CONTRIBUTING.md`, domain vocabulary discovery, and repository testing guidance. Standards and specification reviewers worked separately and serially. Unrelated Research, Deathnote, Gambit, and other feature edits were excluded from Hole scope-expansion findings.

#### Standards

- **H8 — P2: Permanent pipeline tests assert wiring and incidental object replacement.** `tests/domains/hole/part1-pipeline.test.ts:85-100,117-119` assert method call counts and spy invocation order. Lines `101-110` freeze intermediate preparation state. Lines `251-263` require new array/object identities after repeated imports. These assertions reject equivalent implementations that retain the same public results, including combining preparation methods or changing independent late-step order. The assignment forbids permanent wiring/incidental-behavior tests; `docs/TESTING_IMPLEMENTATION.md:41-43` requires typed public results rather than incidental representation. Remove the observation-only spy/order/intermediate-state assertions and incidental identity checks. Keep paired numerical growth, schematic, Gambit, and Tome comparisons, character identity binding, and repeated-result/idempotence checks. The plan's ordering requirement does not exempt test design from this policy.
- **H9 — P3: Tests freeze explanation wording rather than verify effects.** `tests/domains/hole/studies.test.ts:148-152,159-160` require exact prose fragments in Study notes. These checks can fail after a correct wording improvement without any effect or scope change. Remove those prose-substring assertions; keep typed effect keys/scope and extracted numeric/discrete-rule comparisons. The same incidental-copy issue appears in acquisition/reset text assertions at `opal-ledger.test.ts:9-15,41,98-103` and Lantern explanation arrays at `lanterns.test.ts:64-69`. Those literal copies do not verify game semantics, as H12 demonstrates. This is a test-policy finding, not a request to remove the production explanations.

No additional independently actionable abstraction smell, impossible required-domain guard, or feature overengineering was confirmed. The five-role, all-character, 18-Study contracts serve approved outcomes. Focused named-input availability checks establish contracts, not additional captured game branches.

#### Specification

- **H10 — P2: Missing Hole Majik state still produces complete growth rates.** `hole.tsx:1330-1340` checks Village Majik (`Holes[5]`) but omits Hole Majik (`Holes[4]`). Parsing defaults absent Hole Majik levels to zero at `1351-1354`; monument rewards consume Monumental Vibes at `1427-1434`; villager growth consumes those rewards at `1211-1212`. An unknown monument modifier therefore becomes zero inside a numeric, supposedly complete rate. The plan's missing-input rule (`hole-part-1-backend-plan.md:66`) requires unavailable dependent values with reasons. Track this required save input and mark dependent rates/contributors unavailable. Preserve independent progression, Studies, and ledger information. This is a code-proven missing-input branch, not an observed game failure.
- **H11 — P2: Study rates omit required Legend Talent input availability.** `hole.tsx:1343-1347` omits `Spelunk` from required Study inputs. Study progress consumes collectible 16 at `1125,1141-1142`. `updateHole` applies Legend Talent 29 to every collectible at `1498-1502`; `jar.tsx:12-13` includes that multiplier. `legendTalents.tsx:80-82` returns on absent save input without resetting existing levels. Fresh instances can use an unverified zero talent bonus; reused instances can retain prior talent state. Target and eligible neighbors still receive numeric rates without a reason. Track the required Legend Talent save input and mark dependent Study rates and collectible contributions unavailable. Keep independent effects and inactive Study rates. This violates the same plan rule and remains a code-proven branch, not a captured runtime failure.
- **H12 — P2: Bell Opal acquisition names the wrong action.** `hole.tsx:1286` says “Received an Opal from a Bell ring.” The source-backed Bell findings distinguish Ring from Ping: Ring raises permanent bonuses; Ping gives an Opal (`hole-research-brief.md`, “Shallow Caverns: 1–5”, Bell entry). The plan requires each source row to identify its acquisition meaning (`hole-part-1-backend-plan.md:23`). Name Ping in this row and migrate the copied text assertion at `opal-ledger.test.ts:11`. Correct saved counts and ledger arithmetic do not validate this acquisition explanation.

#### H1–H7 assessment

- **H1 resolved:** `villager.tsx:223-317` owns role-specific preparation, availability precedence, contributor construction, and rate multiplication. Hole supplies shared and character-bound inputs.
- **H2 resolved, including required support:** `sneaking.tsx:404-423,464-467` initializes required Pristine Charm records before optional parsing, clears ownership before an absent-input return, and updates saved ownership. `hole.tsx:1545,1578` accesses required Candy Cache directly. Real unowned ownership still yields zero; missing Sneaking input still makes dependent growth unavailable.
- **H3 resolved in production:** `hole.tsx:1033-1042` prepares early shared Study/schematic inputs and cavern Opals. `1044-1065` prepares intrinsic unlocks and reset inspection through the late pipeline entry at `idleonData.tsx:407`. H8 concerns the verification design, not a confirmed production ordering failure.
- **H4 resolved:** `hole.tsx:1158-1176,1263-1264,1573` distinguishes unknown Superbit ownership from false. It preserves known target accrual and independent inactive Studies while making potential neighbor eligibility/rates unavailable.
- **H5 resolved:** `hole.tsx:1190-1200,1328` distinguishes missing Village Majik from zero allocation modifiers. Known counts remain; dependent percentages and contributor values become unavailable.
- **H6 resolved:** `hole.tsx:1214-1215,1328,1335` tracks actual card-save availability and distinguishes missing Gloomie input from verified zero.
- **H7 resolved:** `hole.tsx:379-387` names additive requirement-divisor contributions and explains their combination with collectibles. Refreshed extraction labels retain the observed totals 570%, 1870%, 3420%, and 972%. No complete cavern requirement calculator was added.

#### Evidence, scope, and decision boundary

Active-context records identify Player 3; retained growth, ledger, and Lantern values agree with the recorded revision evidence. The revision reports 17 suites / 444 tests, type checking, and scoped production lint passes. This review did not rerun them; tests remain outside lint coverage.

Fresh and repeated imports use one real save; they do not prove changed-account replacement. No genuine second-account or different-Statue game capture exists. Unobserved gameplay branches remain unobserved. Hand-composed effect and participation extraction expressions do not independently establish consumer semantics.

No additional production ordering defect or scope expansion was confirmed. Jar, Exotic Market 51, and Sneaking initialization support remain necessary to approved outcomes. No forecast, simulation, recommendation, UI, store, endpoint, or game action was added.

#### Standards

- **H13 — P2: Contributor tests still freeze display text and incidental order.** `tests/domains/hole/part1-parameters.test.ts:177-202,210-221` identify expected contributors by their display text and require the complete display-text array in a fixed order. Correct explanation changes or reordered unchanged contributions therefore fail the parameter tests. `docs/TESTING_IMPLEMENTATION.md:41-43` requires calculated public results, not incidental representation. Match expected values through stable owner identities and, where necessary, `sourceIds` or combination. Compare identity sets without ordering and retain every numerical comparison. This is a remaining H9-style policy issue outside the removed prose assertions.
- **H14 — P3: An availability test still requires an unchanged collection reference.** `tests/domains/hole/villager-growth.test.ts:218,229-230` checks other villagers' rate arrays with `toBe`. The required outcome is unchanged public rates, identities, and contributors, not unchanged array identity. Capture those values and compare them with `toEqual`. This violates the same result-focused test policy. H8 removed reference assertions from pipeline tests, but this separate occurrence remains.

These are documented test-policy findings, not heuristic smell claims. No additional independently actionable abstraction smell, impossible initialized-domain guard, or feature overengineering was confirmed. Named missing-save contracts serve an approved requirement; they do not justify guards for missing initialized objects.

#### Specification

- **H15 — P2: Missing collectible Legend Talent still permits complete villager growth.** `data/domain/world-5/hole/hole.tsx:1338,1340-1351` records availability of the required collectible talent value but checks only the outer Legend Talent save for growth. Growth consumes collectible bonuses 4, 10, 12, 22, 29, and 35 at `1205-1206`. `updateHole` applies Legend Talent 29 to every collectible at `1510-1514`; `JarBonus.getBonus` multiplies that input at `jar.tsx:12-13`. A present but truncated talent array can retain the initial or prior talent level (`world-7/legendTalents.tsx:85-89`). Growth rates and the collectible contributor remain numeric despite the unknown multiplier. The plan's line 66 requires each dependent value to be unavailable with a reason. Reuse the existing named talent availability for growth and its collectible contributor. Extend the narrow omission contract to cover these affected character records while retaining independent information.
- **H16 — P2: A missing Compass save still exposes a numeric growth contributor.** `hole.tsx:1341` correctly makes the aggregate growth rate unavailable when Compass input is absent. However, `1588` still passes the Compass calculation, and `villager.tsx:250` publishes its numeric value without a reason. `compass.tsx:637-639` returns before updating absent save input, so that value can be initial zero or prior-account state. A contributor consumer cannot distinguish it from verified current input. Plan line 66 says missing inputs are not zero or complete totals. Carry the existing Compass availability into this contributor and report `null` with a reason. Preserve independent contributors; do not add a missing-domain guard or rewrite Compass.

H15 and H16 are code-proven missing-input defects, not newly observed game failures. They concern genuinely absent saved inputs, not impossible absence of initialized domains.

#### Evidence discrepancy

- **H17 — P3: The handoff states incorrect cavern and external subtotals.** At review time, the continuation's observed-values entry reported 259 cavern-earned and 104 external Opals. The retained ledger artifact records **231 cavern-earned** and **132 external** (`tests/results/hole-opal-ledger-data.json:142-156`). Total earned 363, invested 254, and uninvested 109 agree. The pipeline artifact's value 259 is the extracted Tome score (`hole-part1-pipeline-data.json:154-156`), not the cavern-earned subtotal. The selected H17 revision corrects that narrative. This reporting discrepancy does not demonstrate incorrect production ledger arithmetic.

### Selected H20 revision attempt and mapping blocker

**Historical record:** This section preserves the blocked attempt, exact failure, and evidence that invalidated its Measurement premise. The supervisor's later decision and corrected result follow below.

**Authority and boundary (2026-10-14):** The supervisor selected H20 only under the reusable revision action. Work and delegation stayed serial. The first verification exposed a dependency-mapping conflict. The supervisor then directed the worker to retain attempted edits as a blocked, unaccepted draft, record evidence, and stop without choosing a corrected contract. A fresh authorized worker must resolve that decision before further implementation or review.

- **Attempted changes, not accepted:** Hole parsing tracks `Holes[11][26]` and `Holes[11][28]` independently through named availability flags. Separate reasons reach the draft `schematic:48` and `measurement:0` growth contributors and dependent character rates. Schematic 48 requires counter 26 only when built; known disabled ownership keeps its zero contribution. Broad schematic and Measurement availability still governs independent owners. The draft currently adds a counter-28 gate to Measurement 0, but the evidence below does not establish that dependency.
- **Boundary checks:** Added six real-parse contract cases: missing row 11; missing only counter 26; missing only counter 28; present zero counter 26; present zero counter 28; and known disabled schematic 48 with missing counter 26. Checks cover all five villagers and 11 imported character contexts, stable contributor identities, reasons, independent values, progression, and exact baseline restoration. The disabled schematic case uses an initialized-owner override, not a fabricated saved ownership branch. Whole-row omission permits other dependent row-11 results to become unavailable. These are boundary contracts, not additional captured game parity.
- **Actual verification:** `yarn test tests/domains/hole tests/domains/tome --runInBand` ran after all initial edits. Result: **16 suites passed, one failed; 479 tests passed, one failed; 17 suites / 480 tests total**, in 264.117 seconds. Only the new present-zero kill case failed. It expected `59.49152542372882` and received `308.2993627118644`. No confirmation rerun, follow-up test, lint, build, formatter, or full type check ran. All retained numerical comparisons remain unchanged.
- **Decisive generated identity:** [`MeasurementRepo.ts:11–18`](../../data/domain/data/MeasurementRepo.ts#L11-L18) defines Measurement index 0 with quantity type `"3"`, named **Tome score**, and the Villager EXP bonus. Measurement index 0 is not quantity type 0. Generated records stayed read-only.
- **Website dispatch:** [`Measurement.getMeasurementQTYfound`](../../data/domain/world-5/hole/hole.tsx#L484-L499) dispatches by the generated quantity type. Type 0 reads Gloomie kills; type 3 reads Tome score and divides it by 2,500 for its multiplier. This is retained versioned source evidence, not a new 1.22 capture or runtime observation.
