## 1. What The Hole does

The Hole is entered from the right side of World 5's Mister Brightside map. Its campfire opens villager features. Polonai opens deeper caverns; other villagers are rescued within them. The loop is: collect resources and Opals, allocate Opals to villager growth, gain levels and unlocks, spend competing resources on improvements, then gain better production and rewards. Active challenges, passive production, character skilling, and account upgrades are different parts of that loop. [P1, W1, G1, G3]

Current scope is **18 released caverns across four biome groups**. `CavernsOwned` returns `min(18, PolonaiLevel)`. Publisher releases corroborate Fountain/Trench as 16/17 and Crystal Glunko Cove as 18. Extra source slots, a fourth monument, Elder/unknown villagers, and future reward descriptions do not establish released content. Historical launch had ten caverns/four villagers. Historical Wisdom news calls it cavern 12; current ordering is **Evertree 12, Wisdom 13**. [G1, G18, W1, W4, P1–P3]

### Ownership and meaning

Most progression lives in account-wide `Holes`, alongside separate `Jars` and account options. However, `Holes[0][characterIndex]` records each character's cavern assignment. Character skill efficiency, combat, inventory, and AFK inputs remain character-specific. A shared cavern progress total does not identify each character's contribution or current activity. [G3, G5, G6, G13, G16, O1]

| Distinction | Meaning for website information |
|---|---|
| Saved progression versus possible reward | A built flag, purchased level, earned Opal, or claimed milestone is owned progression. A static description or unlocked reward is not proof it was earned. [G3, G7, G15] |
| Stored progress versus claimed level | Villager EXP and Study progress can exceed their next requirement before camp UI converts them into levels. Show saved level and claim-ready progress separately. [G3, G5] |
| Stock versus work | Sediment, notes, Rupies, wishes, torches, shapes, and Fountain wallet balances are spendable stocks. Skilling materials gathered toward a layer are dedicated cavern progress, not ordinary inventory stacks. [G6, G9, G10, G13, G16, G17] |
| Saved settings versus actor selection | Harp layout/tune, Bell mode, Study target, and boosted Cove shapes have saved settings. Selected UI tabs, highlighted upgrades, reset target, and active minigame choices are actor-only state. [G3, G9, G10, G12, G16] |
| Imported state versus live state | A save supports an as-recorded view. It does not establish live Fear, court coins, a memory board, pending story rewards, or elapsed production since import. [G6, G7, G12, G14, O1] |

Hypothetical allocations, manual court flags, simulated layouts, or website-selected actors are user inputs, not discovered save fields. Browsing a character row must not imply the website changed that character's game assignment.

## 2. Camp: shared progression and resource competition

### Five villagers and EXP

| Villager | Availability and function |
|---|---|
| **Polonai — Explorer** | Available initially. Levels open caverns, bounded by the released cap. |
| **Kaipu — Engineer** | Rescue in Motherlode, cavern 2. Levels expose schematics; construction separately requires resources. |
| **Cosmo — Conjuror** | Rescue in Bell, cavern 5. Each level supplies one shared Majik point. |
| **Minau — Measurer** | Rescue in Lamp, cavern 7. Levels expose Measurements, each upgraded separately. |
| **Bolaia — Librarian/Cavern Scholar** | Rescue in Evertree, cavern 12. Levels improve Study rate. |

Names/rescue locations are Wiki context; publisher independently confirms Bolaia in cavern 12. Exact rescue flags were not audited. No schematic prerequisite for rescuing villagers was established. [W1, P2, G18]

Opals normally supply **100 EXP/hour each**, or **125** with Opal Dividends, before role/account modifiers. Zero invested Opals means zero ordinary rate in this expression. Rates depend on Majik, Measurements, monument rewards, Bell, collectibles, schematics, and outside-account systems; roles do not share one EXP requirement curve. A purchased villager EXP multiplier is distinct from Majik enhancement. [G1, G3]

Accrual adds `rate × elapsedSeconds / 3600` to saved EXP. The inspected camp handler converts levels for the **selected villager** while its panel is open in The Hole. Therefore enough EXP does not mean the saved unlock already happened. Proposed rows show role, rescue/unlock state, invested Opals, saved level, stored EXP, next requirement, current EXP/hour, and next intrinsic unlock. Browse a role and inspect rate contributors without claiming levels. [G3, G5]

### Opals: sources, allocation, ledger, reset

Cavern sources differ: Well purchases; skilling layer claims; Den score milestones; monument reward offers; Bell Ping; Harp D strings; Lamp's misleadingly named **1000000 Opals** wish, which grants one; Tall jars; Grotto Monarchs; Gambit point milestones; Temple Centurions; Fountain purchases; and Cove Opal Find. External grants/purchases also contribute. Do not model every cavern as the same acquisition action. [G1, G2, G6–G17, W2–W5]

The ledger must distinguish **earned total, invested total, and uninvested supply**, with cavern and external sources. The game's `OpalsOwnedToInvest` reads an actor cache; its refresh caller sums cavern Opals and external grants, then subtracts investments. That cache is not an authoritative saved wallet. External routes include Gem Shop purchases, companions, Grimoire, Compass, Emperor, schematics, Arcane, and Sushi bonuses; current release labels and every grant need reconciliation before attribution. [G1, G3]

Allocation creates genuine competition. Opal Enthusiasm rewards tens invested in a villager; Equal Spread rewards blocks of five in the **least-invested unlocked villager**. Neither establishes a universal best allocation. Hold-down investment is an in-game convenience, not a website control. [G1, G3, G18, P2]

Lamp's **Another Try** costs six wishes and resets **one selected category**: invested Opals, invested Conjuror points, Jar enchantment levels, or Summoning doubler assignments. Opal reset leaves earned Opals, villager levels, and stored EXP intact. Majik reset leaves Cosmo's level intact. Enchantment reset retains discovery level one; doubler reset clears assignments. Wishes spent resetting cannot fund other Lamp benefits. Proposed interaction: inspect ledger and exact reset consequences, never redistribute or execute reset. [G10]

### Kaipu: construction is not unlock

Schematics are one-time account constructions. Available display rows follow Kaipu's level, with three more per level and an extra every fifth level, plus the initial extra row. Stable building ID differs from display order through `HolesInfo[40]`; raw catalog order or index must not become an unlock rule. Source availability caps at 105 rows. Bee String exists in data but Wiki lists it as **Never**; presence is not current player availability. [G1, G3, G18, R1, W1]

Construction rejects already-built entries, checks the real resource/cost, subtracts it, sets the built flag, and consumes the next-schematic Lamp discount. Price can be literal or index-dependent; discount multiplier is `max(0.01, 0.85^stacks)`. Catalog base cost is not necessarily materialized current price. Benefits include buckets, expansion, Den amplifiers, Harp strings, challenge unlocks, production synergies, Studies, later-cavern improvements, and an extra Opal. [G1, G3, R1]

Proposed information: identity, availability requirement, built status, intrinsic effect, actual currency, current price, balance/shortfall, and active discount. Search effects; filter locked, built, or affordable entries. Explain shared-resource competition with Measurements and cavern upgrades beside entries.

### Cosmo: one pool, paths, enhancements, Pocket Divinity

All three Majik trees draw from **one pool**: Cosmo levels plus relevant purchases minus every invested point. Later nodes require the preceding node. Cosmo, Enhance! enables extra enhancement levels; those also consume the same pool. `maxEnhance` describes extra allowance, not necessarily total level cap. Effects are not uniformly linear: Beeg Beeg Forge has a power-of-three branch. [G1, G3]

Hole Majik covers monument benefits, Harp slots, extra wishes, Rupies, and Fountain production. Village Majik covers allocation incentives, construction-based EXP, enhancement, Measurements, Studies, and balanced allocation. Idleon Majik covers Pocket Divinity, forge capacity, multi-resource gains, Ballot Box, Weapon Power, and Equinox. Placeholder Confused Bonus nodes are not released useful upgrades. [G18, R1]

Pocket Divinity stores one or two selected unlocked gods; canonical identity differs from array index. It activates their links account-wide, separate from normal character links. Major/minor callers accept effective Doot or the chosen Pocket god through an **OR**, not duplicate major activation. With effective Doot, Pocket contributes **+15% All Stat per invested level**, or 30% at level two. The tooltip substitutes this benefit for god-selection text, while the underlying link-activation OR remains. All four main stats consume the All Stat contribution. Doot's helper requires the relevant character's Divinity level to reach two, so companion ownership alone is insufficient. Pocket is not useless with Doot. [G1, G3, G4]

Proposed details show pool used/remaining, prerequisites, base/enhanced level and cap, current effective benefits, selected gods, and verified overlap with ordinary links/Doot. Browsing trees is not investing points or choosing gods in-game.

### Minau: all 17 Measurement categories

Each unlocked Measurement spends its mapped cavern resource. Its purchased level supplies a base bonus, strengthened by Lengthmeister, then multiplied by an account statistic. Some base rows saturate rather than scale linearly. The measured stat can grow without another purchase. Show **level, raw measured value and units, multiplier, effective bonus, currency, next cost, and shortfall**, not one unexplained percentage. [G1, G3, G18]

| Measured statistic | All corresponding released effects |
|---|---|
| Account level | Villager EXP; Harp notes |
| Tome score | Bravery maximum sword damage; monument AFK rate |
| Death Note points | Bell Ping rate; Gambit points |
| Slab items | Harp notes; villager EXP; Fountain coin value |
| Crops discovered | Daily Lamp wishes |
| Highest recorded damage | Well bucket rate; Rupie value |
| Total account skill levels | Harp string EXP; Jar production |
| Gloomie kills | Multikill per tier |
| Total Study levels | Rupie value |
| Golem kills | Drop rate |

These groupings cover all **17 rows**. Normalization differs by stat; log-based kills/damage are not raw-count multipliers. Missing Tome/runtime cache inputs must not become false zero progress. Filter by effect or resource and inspect source-stat meaning. [G1, G18]

### Bolaia: targeted and adjacent Studies

Study one unlocked cavern. Target, per-cavern level, and stored progress are saved independently. Gaming Superbit 43 also studies immediate unlocked neighbors at the same rate; it does not spread progress across every cavern. Changing target preserves progress. Rate is progress/hour, with Bolaia, Majik, schematics, collectibles, a stamp, and Fountain inputs. Camp drawing converts sufficient visible-row progress into levels. [G1–G3, G5]

Studies change each cavern's rules: Well expansion; cross-skilling requirements for Motherlode/Hive/Evertree/Trench; Den Golden Hounds; Bravery minimum damage/skips; Bell periodic extra levels; Harp EXP; Lamp wishes; Grotto credited kills; Justice reward window/Opal chance; Jar preferred enchantment; Wisdom attempts/instamatch recovery; Gambit points/attempt preservation; Temple doubled torches; Fountain repeated marbleization; Cove daily discounts. Show target/neighbors, level/progress, next requirement, rate, and actual discrete/scaling effects beside cavern entries. [G2, G18]

### Two separate daily constraints

**Blinding Lantern / Quest90**, sold as 2 Hour Lantern, grants two hours of Hole accrual and villager EXP, excluding skilling-layer progress. Its account-wide daily limit is **12**, checked through `Holes[11][84]`. Consumption directly adds **two lifetime respect hours** to each unlocked released monument and separately adds **7,200 seconds** to pending Hole accrual. Normal elapsed processing advances monument story banks through that accumulator; lifetime respect and story charge remain distinct. Lanterns do not refill every daily action or complete active challenges. Inventory quantity/location remains separate from shared usage. [G5, G10, P3, R1]

**Layer claims** share `OptionsListAccount[318]` across four skilling caverns. Current 1.22 claim source blocks at **six**, whereas Wiki says **five**. Daily rollover resets the counter. Retain this discrepancy until live confirmation; do not silently choose five, give each cavern six, or confuse it with Lantern uses. Exact reset clock remains unknown. [G6, G10, W2–W5]

## 3. All released caverns and proposed website information

Everything described as a player action below happens **in the game**. Website interactions are browsing, detail inspection, searching, filtering, and read-only comparisons. These information needs support the confirmed visibility direction; exact backend contracts and frontend presentation remain for separately authorized work.

### Shared skilling framework

Motherlode, Hive, Evertree, and Trench gather dedicated progress, then manually claim a completed layer for an Opal. Claim resets progress, raises completed count and next requirements, and spends the shared daily allowance. Required resource quantity and required character efficiency are different. The game's displayed efficiency is **0.25 times** `MotherlodeEffBase`, despite that helper serving all four skills. Relevant Studies and Amethyst Heartstone reduce resource requirements across caves. [G1, G6, G18]

For each, proposed information includes layer/depth, gathered/required/remaining resources, displayed efficiency threshold, character-specific relevant efficiency, ready-to-claim status, Opals, and shared allowance. Character comparison is useful; rate/ETA needs real character yield and AFK assumptions. A full progress bar is not an automatic claim. [G6]

### Shared monument framework

Bravery, Justice, and Wisdom have four separate saved concepts: **lifetime credited respect hours**, **manually claimed milestone count**, **stored story-charge seconds**, and **permanent reward levels**. AFK respect grants whole credited hours with bonuses, not fractional elapsed hours. Milestones are sequential claims at 1, 80, 300, 750, 2,000, 5,000, 10,000, and 24,000 hours. Crossing a threshold does not claim it. [G7, G8, G18]

Normal story start requires reward multiplier at least one, snapshots it, and clears the entire story bank—not lifetime hours or rewards. Base growth is one multiplier per 20 banked hours, linear for 48 hours, then diminishing growth rather than a hard cap; modifiers alter both growth and window. Permanent rewards include linear and saturating categories with monument self-bonus, Cosmo, and Fountain effects. Display actual effective benefit and effective cap rather than unboosted text. [G1, G7]

Proposed common details: lifetime hours, claimed/claimable milestone, banked seconds, current story multiplier/window, permanent level/effect table, earned Opals, and saved starting capabilities. Active boards, sword rolls, court currency, and pending rewards are not reconstructible from ordinary save totals. [G7, G12, G14]

### Shallow Caverns: 1–5

**1. Well.** Assign owned buckets to sediments. Negative balance means remaining rock obstruction, not spendable negative stock. Clearing it permits collection; changing upward targets is gated by obstruction. Buckets have individual hourly rates and share target stock. Stock caps depend on sediment and expansions. Schematic-gated auto-expansion spends stock when capped and raises capacity; higher expansion needs its further unlock. Gravel buys escalating-cost Opals. Spending sediment competes with expansion and camp upgrades. Show assignments, obstruction/stock/cap, expansion count/toggle/blockers, bucket and grouped rates, next Opal cost, and gold-bucket effects. Filter obstructed, capped, or spendable bars; inspect rate sources. Offline repeat-expansion behavior is not fully established. [G1, G2, G5, G6, W2]

**2. Motherlode.** Mine dedicated ore, then break a layer under the shared framework. Rescue Kaipu here. Keep current ore, broken layers, and **lifetime ore** separate: lifetime ore survives claims and powers a Well schematic, while layer count powers another synergy. Browse layer and character Mining details. [G6, W1, W2]

**3. Dawg Den.** Charge/reset unlocked amplifiers, then fight until Fear ends the run. Eight amplifiers trade score multiplier against damage, accuracy, healing, size/HP, MP costs, curse/Fear, starting Fear, or earlier Fear. Schematics open amplifiers and stacking benefits. Saved account best **score** earns Opal thresholds; four class-group records are best **kill counts**, which improve buckets, not class scores. Show charges/caps/debuffs, combined multiplier, best score/next threshold, Opals, and Beginner/Warrior/Archer/Mage kill records. Inspect each amplifier and class record. Do not fabricate a live Fear meter or predicted survival. [G1, G5, G6, W2]

**4. Bravery.** Throw swords, choose a chest after a win, then continue or flee. Rewards remain pending until fleeing; defeat loses them. Rethrow only the last resolved sword, replacing its damage and spending a charge. A retelling restores the current battle/checkpoint, not an escape choice. Saved respect/milestones improve starting swords, rethrows, retelling, and damage; remaining rethrows and battle streak also affect active damage. Benefits cover Mining, buckets, Cooking, villagers, Gaming, damage, Bell, monument AFK, own reward scaling, and Opal odds. Show shared monument details, best Last Stand, saved starting capabilities, and baseline damage sources. Inspect milestones/rewards and read loss-versus-bank rules; active rolls and pending chests stay absent. [G1, G7, W2]

**5. Bell.** Select **Ring, Ping, Clean, or Renew**; only selected mode accumulates charge, while separate banks persist. Ring raises random permanent bonuses; Ping gives an Opal; Clean attempts another improvement method; Renew clears Ring bank/count and bonus levels while retaining Ping, methods, improvement levels, and Opals. Renew is not Clean. Six improvement methods spend coins, Mythril, Gaming bits, Eighth Notes, atoms, or green Rupies. Seven current bonus families include Fountain coin gain, despite Wiki's six-row shallow table. Clean failure pity is **linear** in source, not repeated 1.25× compounding implied by text. Show all mode banks/rates/costs, methods/next resource costs, failures/current odds, bonus levels/effects, and explicit Renew consequences. Browse modes and filter improvements by currency or affordability. [G1, G5, G6, W2, P3]

### Glowshroom Tunnels: 6–10

**6. Harp.** Save tune and string layout; buy notes and drag across strings to strum. At least 100% power begins a rewarded strum, consuming **all stored power** as one snapshot. Each slot rewards once; regeneration pauses during strum. Unassigned owned types still provide passive level bonuses. C yields the selected note; D rolls an Opal; E aids power; F awards nearby notes; G grants other types EXP; A awards every known note. B has no established useful effect and Wiki lists its schematic unavailable. Strings gain EXP/levels; ten notes unlock using the highest current note. Domino makes order matter, Variety makes layout diversity matter, and a successful D pluck changes later Opal odds. Show power/hour, saved power/layout/tune, owned types/passives, slots, string EXP, note balances and next cost. Inspect types/notes without presenting one fixed strum outcome or silently inferring transient order. [G1, G9, G18, W1, W3]

**7. Lamp.** Bank daily wishes, unlock wish types, and spend on selected effects. Daily restock is `floor(averageDailyWishes + randomFloat())`: fractional rate is an expectation, not guaranteed extra wishes. Besides resets, wishes buy one Opal, add 12 permanent respect hours to a chosen monument, stack next-schematic discount, boost resources, or supply World 4/5/6/7 benefits. Source applies additional account scaling; base text is not final effect. Knowledge of Future and World 8 descriptions remain unsupported future effects. Show wish stock, expected daily gain, unlock/purchase progression, prices, permanent benefits, discount stacks, and reset categories. Search wishes and inspect costs/consequences; no purchase/reset control. [G1, G10, W3]

**8. Undying Hive.** Catch Fractalfly toward a harvestable layer. Catching efficiency is relevant, not Mining despite the shared helper. Completed layers strengthen Harp via its schematic; current flies do not substitute for layers. Show shared skilling information and owned Harp synergy; inspect Catching capability and requirement details. [G6, G9, W3]

**9. Gloomie Mushroom Grotto.** Kill ordinary mushrooms toward colony quota, then trigger and defeat a separate Monarch for an Opal. Monarch HP grows with cleared colonies. Current colony kills reset; lifetime **credited kills** persist and can include multikill/Study weighting. Schematics turn cleared colonies into drop rate, class EXP, villager EXP, and respawn benefits. Show colony, kill progress/quota, readiness, Monarch HP, lifetime record, Opals, and owned synergy. Browse combat requirements and benefit sources. Statue/class-respawn tips remain secondary guidance, not audited recommendations. [G1, G2, G11, W3, P2, P3]

**10. Justice.** Approve, reject, or dismiss court cases using temporary coins, mental health, popularity, and dismissals. Cases include immediate costs, affordability gates, loans, investments, promises, forced choices, ongoing popularity/coin changes, special dismissal outcomes, and random effects. Story “days” often advance by case transitions, not daily reset. Timmy's sequence can award a permanent achievement. Earned retirement chests yield chosen rewards after court ends; settlement then commits permanent levels/Opals. Benefits cover Catching, Harp, artifacts, villagers, Summoning, class EXP, wishes, monument AFK, self-scaling, and Opal odds. Show common monument progress and saved starting capabilities. Browse/search cases by NPC, currency, or consequence, explaining continuing effects beside immediate deltas. Current case, flags, court wallet, and unsettled rewards remain actor-only; a case browser is not a greedy strategy engine. [G1, G12, G18, W3]

### Underground Overgrowth: 11–15

**11. Jars.** Select production types, let jars form on a saved board, then break them. Ten types produce basic/decent/elegant Rupies, Opal chance, new collectibles, enchantments, white Rupies, a gilded bonus, dark Rupies, or master Rupies. There are **12 currencies**: standard/master balances plus separate white/dark counters. Current holdings gate colors and next jar purchases. Up to three producers and break-all are schematic-gated. Quality scales rewards; gilded jars store a bonus consumed by the next Rupie-paying jar, so order matters. All **40 collectibles** have saved levels: discovery sets one, each enchant adds one repeatedly. First enchant doubles level one; it is not a binary enchant flag. Study's preferred target biases rather than guarantees enchantment. Effects cover currency, production, chances, villagers, skilling requirements, Well/Bell/Harp/Studies/monuments/Gambit, plus explicitly future descriptions. Show board/type/quality, production work/rate, all wallets/gates, next jar cost, pending gilded bonus, collectible levels/effects, and preferred target. Filter missing, enchanted, or benefit categories. Exact offline conversion remains unaudited. [G1, G2, G13, G18, W4]

**12. Evertree.** Chop Everlogs and whittle trunks for Opals under the shared framework. Rescue Bolaia here. Trunk Studies reduce other skilling requirements. Show Choppin efficiency, wood/trunk progress, shared claims, rescue context, and cross-cavern effect; browse character capability. [G6, G18, W1, W4, P2]

**13. Wisdom.** Spend normal story charge on matching cards, use instamatches, clear boards, and choose rewards. Saved milestones/respect/Studies determine starting attempts, gains per clear, and instamatches. Pure-memory mode has distinct rules: 30 attempts, no per-clear gains or instamatches, and a different timer; its entry preserves the normal bank in inspected source. Normal board/attempts/reveals and pending rewards are transient. Benefits cover Choppin, Rupies, Jade, villagers, Farming, drop rate, Gambit, monument AFK, self-scaling, and Opal odds. Show common monument data and saved starting capabilities; inspect normal/pure rules and reward/milestone details. No hidden-card answers or assumed active board. Default to normal-mode information; keep secret-mode descriptions discoverable without selecting that mode by default. [G1, G2, G7, G14, W4, P2]

**14. Gambit.** Six Summoning-style challenges are King survival, Horsey one HP, Bishop reflected damage, Queen all-jeweled cards, Castle one lane, and Noob gray slimes. Schematics unlock later challenges. Saved best survival seconds drive points; current multipliers can raise total points without new records. Normally a shared daily flag limits entry; Study can probabilistically preserve the attempt, not guarantee two attempts. Summoning doubler assignments are separate saved rewards reset through Lamp. Nineteen reward slots include sixteen named current descriptions and three future/unknown effects: doublers, Opals, Flurbos, resources, stones, essence, ribbons, coins, Owl/Roo, Construction, Snail Mail, stealth, bones, bubbles, Spelunking, and Research. Do not claim all nineteen released useful effects. Source score parenthesization differs from Wiki shorthand; ribbon extra-rank applicability has exclusions, including rank 20+ in publisher context. Show challenge rules/unlocks/records/points, base/effective total, contributors, next reward, daily flag, and doublers/assignments. Browse rewards and challenge details, not predicted survival. [G2, G15, G18, W4, P2, P3]

**15. Temple.** Kill Ancient Golems for torches. Bonfire spends them through three actions: **Illuminate** improves search, **Search** can awaken a Centurion, **Amplify** improves active-only respawn. Successful awakening resets Illuminate immediately; death increments Sanctum and grants an Opal. Torch doubling has shared bonus inputs. There is no separately established bonfire level/currency. Show Sanctum, torches, action prices/effects/affordability, current displayed Search chance, reset consequence, active-only label, torch sources, Opals, and intrinsic drop context. Inspect actions or drop details. Search purchase ordering and randomness prevent guaranteed Opal timelines. [G2, G16, W4]

### Sentinel Passages: 16–18

**16. Fountain.** Fill bars, generate random eligible currencies onto stacks, collect them, buy upgrades, and marbleize eligible upgrades. Distinguish nine-currency **spendable wallet**, saved **uncollected stack arrays/royal flags**, and actor stack images. Overflow partially credits wallet. Ignored currencies change eligibility; desired currency changes value. Effective capacity is capped at **16 positions × 50 height**, even if purchased levels grow. Later currency families use different value scaling. [G2, G17, G18, W5]

Three released upgrade pages contain **60 slots**: Regular covers currencies, capacity/overflow, fill/active speed, Bravery/Cosmo, golden buckets and global benefits; Yellow adds bills, royal stacks, marble, desire/ignore, Justice/Studies/Harp and global benefits; Green adds stack currencies, luck/ducks, Wisdom, Measurement costs, Jar enchantments and global benefits. Displayed water navigates upgrades, not exclusive production. Red Water's present multiplier description does not establish a released red page; Oldmoney is not a generated tenth currency. [G2, G17, G18, W5, P3]

Marble **amount**, base upgrade **level**, and per-upgrade marbleization **level** differ. Study enables repeated marbleization; excluded upgrades remain ineligible. Collection rolls permanent per-currency luck; duck-bar completion rolls a duck rather than guaranteeing one. Lucky counts/ducks improve value, so pending stack valuation can change during collection. Source also contains increasing-cost direct Opal purchases; later currency reachability needs reconciliation. [G2, G17]

Proposed information includes every wallet, pending stack/royal state and snapshot value, ignore/desire setting, rate/bar progress, effective capacity/occupancy/overflow, all upgrade prerequisites/currency/cost/effects, marble amount/eligibility/levels, luck/ducks, and Opals. Browse pages, filter locked/affordable/marble-eligible upgrades, inspect value sources. Do not equate upgraded capacity with effective capacity or promise a specific next currency/duck.

**17. Bottomless Trench.** Fish dedicated Trench Fish, then Cast completed depths for Opals. Higher depths raise requirements; Studies feed cross-skilling reductions, while a schematic ties depth to Fountain value. Show Fishing capability and the shared layer information; inspect synergy and remaining daily claims. Wiki's missing efficiency table does not mean source lacks a threshold. [G1, G2, G6, G18, W5]

**18. Crystal Glunko Cove.** Kill Crystal Glunkos and pick up **12 special shapes**, recorded in account balances rather than ordinary inventory. Blue and purple boosted selections are saved separately. **24 upgrade slots** include local DR, AFK, multikill, respawn and pickup effects, currency-sensitive collectors, costs, global bonuses, an Opal, and rank-15 Cooking ribbon; IDK Broh remains explicitly future/unknown. Source overrides normal DR here, and normal stats may still be inputs. Show Cove-local outputs with local labels, not ordinary account DR/AFK values. Study discounts a number of first daily purchases by 15%; the counter is not a daily purchase cap. Show shapes/boosted targets, kills, upgrade levels/currencies/prices/effects/shortfalls, local/global scope, discount allowance, and earned rewards. Filter blue/purple, local/global, affordable, or owned entries and inspect each stat source. Digit/log-based collectors need exact source semantics at powers of ten. [G2, G16, G18, W5, P3]

## 4. Confirmed first-pass outcomes and boundaries

**Confirmed baseline:** A player can understand the entire feature's current progression and requirements, distinguish owned from possible benefits, inspect verified capability, stats and next costs, see resource competition, and recognize shared limits. Descriptions belong beside relevant entries, not in a mandatory enormous wiki. Current deterministic arithmetic is useful even when it consumes cross-account modifiers; inaccurate inputs must remain unknown, not fake zero.

1. **Visibility and verified capability.** Current progression, effective benefits, costs, shortfalls, and verified capability comparisons are the focus. Existing source-backed rate information may describe recorded state, but an efficiency/rate engine, simulation, hypothetical allocation planner, strategy helper, and recommendations are outside this first pass.
2. **Released and locked content.** Show everything in the released feature, including locked entries, by default. Where reasonable, provide a **Show unlocked** checkbox that restricts the view. No secret-mode view is selected by default; secret-mode descriptions remain discoverable with other released information. Source-only future placeholders are still not released content.
3. **Snapshot-based information.** Do not advance saved production, balances, readiness, or progress using elapsed time between snapshot/cloud-save and current time. Show recorded state and verified current-state calculations without implying live freshness. Forecasts are not a first-pass focus; no countdown or timer is authorized by this scope confirmation.

Read-only visibility never authorizes spending, constructing, allocating, equipping, assigning characters, claiming, starting challenges, opening jars, ringing/cleaning/renewing, or advancing the game.

## 5. Factual gaps and current coverage

These gaps do not reduce scope to existing parser coverage or prevent formalizing outcomes. Separately authorized backend work must verify data ownership, source inputs, and affected calculations; frontend work must verify labels, presentation, and actual player-facing behavior. Unverified outputs must not be presented as established facts.

- **Live parity and rescue:** No connected live account or Hole screen; exact rescue flags, entry enforcement, and source-only availability need confirmation.
- **Daily/reset semantics:** Reconcile source-six/Wiki-five layer claims before presenting an authoritative remaining-claims total. Twelve Lantern uses are a separate confirmed source/publisher limit. Reset clock/timezone and imported timestamp meaning are not first-pass countdown requirements.
- **Rates and offline behavior:** Verify character capability inputs and any displayed rates, Well expansion behavior, Jar board semantics, active Fountain modifiers, and Lantern effects where relevant to current-state information. Detailed elapsed-time bridges and forecast behavior are not first-pass requirements.
- **State/save mapping:** Complete persistent-state mapping and export behavior across accounts; do not invent current minigame boards, pending rewards, actor selections, or contribution history.
- **Static completeness:** Existing catalogs cover many families, including buildings, Majik, Measurements, Studies, Bell, jars/collectibles, Gambit, Fountain, and shapes. No specific missing static field was demonstrated by the bounded catalog search. Full value/description/current-version parity remains unaudited; no catalog-lag conclusion follows from nominal counts. [R1]
- **Effective/downstream semantics:** Resolve all modifier/cap inputs, other god-link alternatives, price currencies, stochastic branches, and external additive/multiplicative callers before claiming calculator parity. Pocket's Doot All Stat alternative is verified in source, but full personalized aggregation is not. Clean pity, Bell's seventh bonus, Fountain effective caps, and Gambit score wording show why text alone is insufficient. [G1, G2, G4, G6, G15, G17]

Existing backend collections and source-calculation history are a footprint, not exhaustive parser or product authority. Closed issues **#384, #364, and #357** record prior selected correctness work. The earlier bounded docs/tracker search found no broad Hole product approval; the human's first-pass direction is now recorded in this brief, not a published delivery specification. The hidden UI remains excluded entirely. [R2]

## 6. Checks actually observed

The connected `http://127.0.0.1:3100/status` request timed out after 5,001 ms. Offline status reported `source: offline`, `cdpConnected: true`, `injected: true`, `gameReady: true`, and paused virtual time. An unsupported `/game-version` route returned 404; that was not runtime failure. [O1]

This exact read-only access expression succeeded:

```js
({
  version: idleon.getGameVersion(),
  sourceLengths: [
    idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_579._customBlock_Holes.toString().length,
    idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_579._customBlock_Holes2.toString().length
  ],
  holeSaveArrays: idleon.getAttr("Holes").length
})
// version: { title: "Jelly_Operator", version: "1.22" }
// sourceLengths: [45096, 23242]; holeSaveArrays: 35
```

Representative anonymized observations included villager levels `[20,48,68,30,150,0,0,0]`, 18 unlocked caverns under the source cap, 17 Measurements, 80 Majik points owned/66 invested, zero Lantern uses, and Study target index 12. Harp power `48885.69767204844`, wish stock `3128`, colony clears `12`, current/lifetime kills `1901637656.7288694 / 7841653999.9918375`, and Justice hours/claimed milestones `[24090,8]` demonstrate distinct state meanings. [O1, G3, G9, G11, G12]

Startup save contained stringified `Holes` and `Jars`, plus `OptLacc`; examined data had 35 Hole rows, 120 jar entries, saved Fountain stack arrays, marble amount `1204194`, and per-upgrade marble level three. Pure local arithmetic on the captured unboosted monument formula gave 20 hours → 1 multiplier and 72 hours → `2.6668546920110243`. These are state-access and expression-reading smoke results, **not game-calculator validation**, complete-save proof, typical account values, or live surface verification. No personal identifiers are retained. [O1, G7, G13, G17]

## Sources and durable evidence ledger

All source inspection occurred on 2026-10-12 against offline original game **1.22 Jelly_Operator**. Runtime reads used `POST http://127.0.0.1:3101/exec` with `{ "expression": "..." }`; source functions were not executed. Some callers were inspected only in the [matching extracted original file](../../../IdleonWikiBot/codefiles/idleon1.22.txt), in the sibling WikiBot checkout, not bundled here. Exact function/branch anchors support reinspection. Reported file offsets are **provisional** because reports mix bytes and characters; search anchors take precedence. Runtime-string offsets are characters, not file lines. No scratch-report links are required.

### Original source and observations

- **O1 — Access and limits:** [Offline README](../../sub-projects/idleon-offline/README.md), [server](../../sub-projects/idleon-offline/server.cjs), and [extractor accessor](../../tests/helpers/game-data-extractor.js):73. Exact expressions `idleon.getGameVersion()`, `idleon.findFunction("Holes")`, `idleon.findFunction("Holes2")`, and access packet above; both functions resolved to `ActorEvents_579`. Read-only `/status` and `/cloud-save` establish observed offline/input context only.
- **G1 — Primary helper:** `idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_579._customBlock_Holes.toString()`. Matching file assignment `m._customBlock_Holes = function` at character offset **21,565,698**. Branches: `CavernsOwned`, `VillagersOwned`, `VillagerExpPerHour`, `VillagerExpREQ`, `OpalsOwnedToInvest`, `LeastOpalsInVillager`, `SchematicsAvailable`, `CanWeAffordCreate`, `BuildCost`, `B_UPG`, `CosmoPtsLeft`, `CanWeEnhanceCosmo`, `EnhanceMaxLV`, `CosmoBonusQTY`, `PocketDivOwned`, `MeasurementsOwned`, `MeasurementCost`, `MeasurementBaseBonus`, `MeasurementQTYfound`, `MeasurementMulti`, `MeasurementBonusTOTAL`, `BolaiaStudyRate`; cavern branches below.
- **G2 — Extended helper:** `idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_579._customBlock_Holes2.toString()`. Matching assignment `m._customBlock_Holes2 = function` at character offset **21,648,230**. Branches `StudyReqForBolaia`, `StudyBolaiaBonuses`, `HolezBucketGoldOwned`, `HolezBucketGoldMult`, `XtraMushKillz`, `JarRupieTypeGiven`, `CanWeBreakAll`, `JarCollectibleBonus`; later-cavern branches below.
- **G3 — Camp actions:** `idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_670.prototype._event_villagers.toString()` (matching character offset **24,165,329**) and `._customEvent_CavernStuffz.toString()`. Trigger `"d"` refreshes Opal ledger; villager handler invests/builds/enhances/selects Studies/gods. Selected-villager level conversion is in `_event_12` near **24,220,161**.

Capacity clamps are **16** and **50**. Opal cost is `100*(1+n)^1.5*4.8^n`, paid from `[9][30+min(9,floor(n/5))]` for existing Fountain Opals n. Purchase caller near byte **24,079,736** subtracts wallet and increments `[7][15]`; generated currency IDs remain 0–8. Fountain has three twenty-entry pages; Cove RandoListo2 rows 11–14 own names/effects/increments/cost growth.
